        assert _page(e, t)["center"]["label"] == "OSIMERTINIB", t
    for q, label in (("Which trials evaluate resistance linked to osimertinib?", "OSIMERTINIB"),
                     ("Which trials evaluate antibody-drug conjugate trastuzumab emtansine?",
                      "TRASTUZUMAB EMTANSINE")):
        ents = e.kg.link_entities(q)
        assert [x["label"] for x in ents if x["type"] == "Drug"] == [label], q
        assert not any(x["type"] == "Refused" for x in ents), q


# Codex review m32 (prepared before the round): the map takes one whole name
def test_the_map_draws_only_a_whole_stored_name():
    import app
    e = app.Engine()
    for t in ("osimertinib", " Osimertinib? ", "OSIMERTINIB", "ＯＳＩＭＥＲＴＩＮＩＢ",
              "osimertinib​", "Tarceva®", "nct00200395", "chembl553", "Merck & Co", "EGFR", "egfr"):
        assert e.explore(t).get("center"), t
    # a second name, a qualifier, a dose, a partner, a dye, a sentence: not drawn
    for t in ("osimertinib EGFR", "osimertinib 80 mg", "trastuzumab linked at lysine residues to eFluor 450",
              "trastuzumab conjugated to a new dye", "osimertinib linked to EGFR mutations",
              "Which trials evaluate osimertinib?", "the osimertinib", "osimertinib osimertinib",
              "osimertinib and bevacizumab", "trastu+zumab", "trastu$zumab", "trastuzumabß",
              "AstraZeneca +", "Pfi+zer", "Roche $"):
        r = e.explore(t)
        assert "center" not in r and (r.get("not_one_name") or r.get("not_found") or r.get("refused")), t
    # a symbol or non-Latin letter the dictionary key drops still counts as more than the name
    for t in ("trastuzumab ☢", "☢ trastuzumab", "interferon α", "Roche ☢",
              "trastu☢zumab", "trastuzumab-α", "interferon-γ", "trastuzumab٣",
              "trastuzumab😀"):
        assert "center" not in e.explore(t), t
    # a node's own label is a name too; a label shared with a drug alias asks to choose
    assert e.explore("Li Zhang Md")["center"]["type"] == "Investigator"
    assert e.explore("Armando Santoro Md")["center"]["type"] == "Investigator"
    # any refused part refuses the whole input, even beside a plain name
    for t in ("Mvasi", "osimertinib, Mvasi", "Mvasi and bevacizumab", "osimertinib, [89Zr] trastuzumab"):
        assert e.explore(t).get("refused"), t


def test_a_key_shared_by_several_drugs_is_not_decided_by_spelling():
    import app
    e = app.Engine()
    # medi4736 is filed under durvalumab (ChEMBL) and, by a registry synonym, atezolizumab;
    # 5-fluorouracil also under capecitabine; ak112 under envafolimab and ivonescimab. The
    # question linker reads none of them; the map reads a small molecule's ChEMBL names,
    # which name the right molecule, and no antibody synonym at all
    right = {"crlx101": "CHEMBL4802201", "dha-paclitaxel": "CHEMBL4297441",
             "Dha paclitaxel": "CHEMBL4297441"}
    for t in ("medi4736", "MEDI-4736", "MEDI4736", "5-fluorouracil", "5 - fluorouracil", "ak112",
              "AK-112", "peginterferon alfa-2b", "peg-interferon alfa-2b", "crlx101", "PD-1 mAb",
              "dha-paclitaxel", "Dha paclitaxel"):
        r = e.explore(t)
        assert r.get("center", {}).get("id") == right.get(t), (t, r)
        ents = e.kg.link_entities(t)
        assert not [x for x in ents if x["type"] == "Drug"], (t, ents)
    # a drug's own preferred name still chooses it
    assert e.explore("NICOTINE")["center"]["id"] == "CHEMBL3"
    assert e.explore("(+/-)NICOTINE")["center"]["id"] == "CHEMBL440464"
    assert e.explore("fluorouracil")["center"]["label"] == "FLUOROURACIL"


def test_the_map_reads_stored_names_under_one_rule():
    import app
    e = app.Engine()
    # several live nodes under one name: the reader chooses, whatever the spelling
    for t, ids in (("ABT-510", {"CHEMBL386115", "CHEMBL262489"}), ("ABT 510", {"CHEMBL386115", "CHEMBL262489"}),
                   ("FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE F 18", {"CHEMBL2030434", "CHEMBL497613"}),
                   ("FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE-F18", {"CHEMBL2030434", "CHEMBL497613"})):
        r = e.explore(t)
        assert "center" not in r and {c["id"] for c in r["choose"]} == ids, (t, r)
    # a trademark sign is dropped before compatibility folding never turns into "TM"
    for t in ("Tarceva", "Tarceva®", "Tarceva™"):
        assert e.explore(t)["center"]["id"] == "CHEMBL1079742", t
    assert e.explore("trastuzumab™")["center"]["id"] == "CHEMBL1201585"
    # registry-only synonyms with a wrong identity are not read by the map
    for t in ("bupivacaine hydrochloride", "irinotecan liposome", "nanoliposomal irinotecan",
              "doxorubicin hydrochloride liposome", "ropeginterferon alfa-2b", "13-cis retinoic acid"):
        assert e.explore(t).get("registry_name"), t
    # MAGE-A3 is the target's name; the vaccine's synonym is an antibody/protein synonym
    for t in ("MAGE-A3", "MAGEA3"):
        assert e.explore(t)["center"]["id"] == "ENSG00000221867", t
    # an antibody's or protein's ChEMBL synonym is not read: it may be a biosimilar code
    for t in ("CT-P13", "Zessly", "ABP 980", "HLX04", "MK-3475", "MEDI4736"):
        assert e.explore(t).get("biologic_synonym"), t
    # trade names of other products filed under a molecule are refused, here and in questions
    for t in ("Inflectra", "Remsima", "Herwenda", "Equidacent", "Zarzio", "Pelgraz", "Myocet",
              "Fyarro", "Megace es", "Jelmyto", "Locteron"):
        assert e.explore(t).get("refused"), t
        assert any(x["type"] == "Refused" for x in e.kg.link_entities(f"Which trials evaluate {t}?")), t
    for t, did in (("Remicade", "CHEMBL1201581"), ("Herceptin", "CHEMBL1201585"), ("Megace", "CHEMBL1201139"),
                   ("Kadcyla", "CHEMBL1743082"), ("Keytruda", "CHEMBL3137343"), ("Neulasta", "CHEMBL1201568")):
        assert e.explore(t)["center"]["id"] == did, t
    # a company key matches only input with nothing the key would drop
    assert e.explore("Merck Co")["center"]["id"] == e.explore("Merck & Co")["center"]["id"]
    for t in ("Merck + Co", "Pfizer$", "AstraZeneca +"):
        assert "center" not in e.explore(t), t

Root independently captured 77 baseline requests, four further data requests and 21 should-fix/control requests: **102 requests**. The parallel lanes have additional independent HTTP captures. [Baseline responses](root_http.json), [summary](root_confirmation.json), [data confirmation](root_data_confirmation.json), [should-fix confirmation](root_shouldfix_confirmation.json).
+- Checked **all 6,469 live centers against independent SQLite results**, covering **22 relation/direction/type/role combinations**, with **zero oracle errors**. All **19 DB assertions** pass, including private retirement, malformed-property, numeric and 1,001-drug chunking fixtures. [Oracle](contract/db_validate.json), [assertions](contract/db_assertions.json).
+- Executed the actual served JavaScript in a Node VM DOM/fetch harness, including real HTTP choice clicks, demos, new reply states, escaping and history. This is **not a browser rendering test**. An attempted actual browser session timed out; a fresh CUA inventory reported no available apps/browsers, an unavailable native pipe and an unavailable browser access host. No visual-layout or painted-browser result is claimed.
+- The source review, fixtures, original-script reuse and immutable-file verification are documented below and in the linked ledgers. Source fixtures use in-memory SQLite copies; read-only audit changes stay under m33. The code-change condition involving KOA Studio/KhOpenApi does not arise because no tracked implementation is edited.
+
+## Code blocker
+
+### M33-B1 — refusal and admission use different equivalence rules
+
+`src/app.py:59-73` includes trade names even when the question linker's `_OTHER_PRODUCT_NAMES` declares them a different product. `Engine.explore` at `src/app.py:139-150` first asks the linker to refuse the input, then admits a stored name through `_sig`. The latter ignores punctuation, spacing and trademark signs. The former depends on token windows and clause boundaries (`src/query.py:788-805,874-896`) and folds trademark signs differently (`src/query.py:117-118`). Thus an empty/non-refused linker result is not proof that the stored name is safe to draw.
+
+All following pairs were independently reproduced over HTTP. Plain controls return `refused`; the variants return `center`:
+
+| Plain, refused input | Exact variant drawn | Incorrect center |
+|---|---|---|
+| `Myocet` | `Myocet™`, `My/o/cet`, `My ocet` | `CHEMBL359744` — DOXORUBICIN HYDROCHLORIDE |
+| `Inflectra` | `Inf/lectra` | `CHEMBL1201581` — INFLIXIMAB |
+| `Equidacent` | `Equi/dacent` | `CHEMBL1201583` — BEVACIZUMAB |
+| `Fyarro` | `Fy/arro` | `CHEMBL413` — SIROLIMUS |
+| `Megace ES` | `Megace/ES` | `CHEMBL1201139` — MEGESTROL ACETATE |
+
+For example, `_sig('Myocet') == _sig('Myocet™') == _sig('My/o/cet') == 'myocet'`. This is not an extra qualifier absent from the stored name. It is a **known refused product admitted through another path**, directly violating the refusal guarantee. `_OTHER_PRODUCT_NAMES` already records that these brands denote other products; no new external product interpretation is needed to establish the code inconsistency.
+
+The systematic sweep covers **38 stored spellings × 12 forms = 456 cases**. **248 forms across 37 stored spellings draw a center**; 201 are refused and seven return an excluded-source reply. This is one failure family, not 248 separate blockers. [Sweep and exact forms](attacks/blocked_brand_variants.json), [counts](attacks/blocked_brand_summary.json), [independent attack HTTP](attacks/http_attack_reproductions.json), [root corroboration](root_confirmation.json).
+
+The necessary invariant is that equivalent spellings of a known refused stored name remain refused when the map admits names under `_sig`. Fixing only a few displayed variants would leave the differing comparison rules in place. No implementation change was made during this review.
+
+## Data blockers, separate from code
+
+The following are **exact stored trade-name records that the map still reads**. They are not registry-only synonyms, biologic development codes, or free-text question leaks. Each response displays the indicated single-ingredient node and its relations as the identity of the named multi-ingredient medicine.
+
+Every source record below has **`source: null`**, **`alias_type: "trade_name"`** in `data/processed/resolved.json:1`, at `dictionary.index[<key>]`. The `null` is preserved literally here: the provenance is the ChEMBL-derived `tradeNames` branch in `src/normalize.py:174-175`, not a literal `source: "ChEMBL"` field. [Exact records](attacks/all_alias_source_records.json), [root source/HTTP confirmation](root_data_confirmation.json), [additional root HTTP](root_http.json).
+
+| Finding | Exact input; alias; dictionary key | Drawn node | Independent identity reference |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| M33-D1 | `Oraqix`; `Oraqix`; `oraqix` | `CHEMBL79` LIDOCAINE | The product contains **lidocaine and prilocaine**, rather than lidocaine alone. [Official DailyMed label](https://dailymed.nlm.nih.gov/dailymed/lookup.cfm?setid=b501d239-923b-48b2-82ed-6c5c253a67e2). |

[HPRA product record](https://www.hpra.ie/find-a-medicine/for-human-use/authorised-medicines/details/28877). |

+An ingredient's presence in a product does not make the product's whole name an identity alias for that ingredient under this review's distinct-product guarantee. These records need a product-aware curation/admission decision. Keeping them as trusted whole-name aliases still draws the wrong product even if M33-B1 is repaired.
+
+**Candidate not promoted to a finding:** `Acticin` draws TRETINOIN, while an FDA name-review table identifies an Acticin permethrin cream. However, separate historical FDA NDA 20-400 records explicitly call a tretinoin gel Acticin. That counterevidence prevents an unqualified claim that this stored alias is wrong. It is excluded from the blocker count. [Historical FDA statistical review](https://www.accessdata.fda.gov/drugsatfda_docs/nda/98/20400_statr_micror.pdf), [pharmacology review](https://www.accessdata.fda.gov/drugsatfda_docs/nda/98/20400_phrmr.pdf), [audit counterevidence record](root_identity_counterevidence.json). Other screened but unproven candidates are likewise not counted.

+## New should-fix items and question-box checks
+
+**M33-S1 — the preferred-name question repair can select the wrong stereochemical record.** `(+/-)NICOTINE` by itself links and maps to its preferred-name node, **CHEMBL440464**. But **`What trials evaluate (+/-)NICOTINE?`** and **`What does (+/-)NICOTINE target?`** now link **CHEMBL3 NICOTINE**; the parent query module abstained. Symbol/token splitting leaves a shorter `NICOTINE` window, which the new preferred-name selection at `src/query.py:887-893` accepts. The three question boxes use the resulting wrong evidence; the map refuses those full questions as `not_one_name`. This is a newly introduced question-linker regression, reported as should-fix under the given scope. [Parent/current comparison and HTTP](contract/racemic_nicotine_regression.json), [root corroboration](root_shouldfix_confirmation.json).
+
+The **99-shared-key / 708-case** question comparison contains **12 changes**: ten intended preferred-name recoveries and the two racemic-nicotine question regressions. All **36 demo examples** are unchanged and their actual JavaScript callbacks pass. All **38 added product keys**, bare and in two question wrappers, are refused; **114 question-box HTTP calls** return no evidence for that product matrix. Those successful controls do not cover the map's M33-B1 equivalent-spelling bypass. [Shared-key comparison](contract/shared_key_question_comparison.json), [demos](contract/demo_previous_comparison.json), [new product checks](contract/product_questions.json).
+
+**M33-S2 — a permitted source can be hidden by an earlier excluded-source record.** `Darzalex`, `Tepezza`, `Tevimbra`, `Winrevair`, `Privigen` return `biologic_synonym`; `Lmx 4` returns `registry_name`, even though a `trade_name` record is retained in the drug's alias list. The upstream index deduplicates a spelling before the map classifies its source; `Engine.__init__` reads that index rather than all source records. This loses a permitted trade-name source and makes the reply's explanation inaccurate. It is a recall/source-classification defect, not a wrong-node blocker. The screening list of 97 source discrepancies includes intentional curated removals and is **not 97 proven defects**. [Records and actual replies](attacks/alias_storage_discrepancies.json), [root HTTP](root_shouldfix_confirmation.json).

+"""Independent round-33 HTTP confirmation. No production writes or model calls."""
+import json
+import sys
+import time
+import unicodedata
+import urllib.error
+import urllib.request
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+BASE = 'http://127.0.0.1:8933'
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'src'))
+from app import _sig
+
+
+def post(text, route='/api/explore', **extra):
+    req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + route,
+        data=json.dumps({'text': text, **extra}).encode(),
+        headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
+    start = time.perf_counter()
+    try:
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=120) as response:
+            status, body = response.status, response.read()
+    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
+        status, body = e.code, e.read()
+    return {'text': text, 'route': route, 'status': status,
+            'seconds': time.perf_counter() - start, 'body': json.loads(body)}
+
+
+def main():
+    cases = [
+        'trastu+zumab', 'trastu$zumab', 'trastu=zumab', 'trastuzumabß',
+        'trastuzumabγ', 'trastuzumab☢', 'trastuzumab²', 'AstraZeneca +',
+        'Merck + Co', 'Pfizer$', 'Pfi+zer', 'Roche $', 'Merck & Co',
+        'ABT-510', 'ABT 510', 'MAGE-A3', 'MAGEA3', 'ctla-4', 'CTLA4',
+        'FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE F 18', 'FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE-F18', 'Tarceva',
+        'Tarceva™', 'Tarceva®', 'Tarceva©', 'Tarceva℠',
+        'bupivacaine hydrochloride', 'irinotecan liposome',
+        'irinotecan liposome injection', 'nanoliposomal irinotecan',
+        'doxorubicin hydrochloride liposome', 'ropeginterferon alfa-2b',
+        '13-cis retinoic acid', 'thymosin α1', 'ifn-α-2b', 'interferon α-2b',
+        'trastuzumab™', 'AstraZeneca™', 'CT-P13', 'ABP 980', 'Zessly',
+        'MK-3475', 'MEDI4736', 'Roche', 'Kadcyla',
+        'Myocet', 'Myocet™', 'My/o/cet', 'My ocet',
+        'Inflectra', 'Inf/lectra', 'Equidacent', 'Equi/dacent',
+        'Fyarro', 'Fy/arro', 'Megace ES', 'Megace/ES',
+        'Oraqix', 'Actikerall', 'Betnesol-n', 'Depakote',
+        'osimertinib, Mvasi', 'osimertinib EGFR',
+        'chembl1201585', 'ensg00000146648', 'nct00200395',
+        'CHEMBL1201585 +', 'ＣＨＥＭＢＬ１２０１５８５',
+        'trastuzu\u200bmab', 'trastuzu\u200dmab', 'trastu\u202ezumab',
+        'trastuzumab\ufe0f', '', 'x' * 501,
+    ]
+    rows = [post(t) for t in cases]
+    rows += [post('What trials evaluate NICOTINE?', r,
+                  **({'answer': 'Nicotine is evaluated in trials.'} if r != '/api/ask' else {}))
+             for r in ['/api/ask', '/api/structured', '/api/check']]
+    (OUT / 'root_http.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+    summary = [{'text': r['text'], 'status': r['status'],
+        'center': r['body'].get('center', {}).get('id'),
+        'keys': list(r['body']),
+        'choose_ids': [c['id'] for c in r['body'].get('choose', [])]}
+        for r in rows]
+    result = {'request_count': len(rows),
+        'page_equals_worktree': urllib.request.urlopen(BASE).read() == (ROOT / 'web/try.html').read_bytes(),
+        'signature_controls': [{'original': t, 'variant': v, 'sig_original': _sig(t),
+            'sig_variant': _sig(v), 'same': _sig(t) == _sig(v)}
+            for t, v in [('Myocet','Myocet™'), ('Myocet','My/o/cet'),
+                         ('Inflectra','Inf/lectra'), ('Equidacent','Equi/dacent')]],
+        'cases': summary,
+        'browser': {'rendered_test_completed': False,
+            'reason': 'CUA createBrowserTab timed out; subsequent getState returned no apps/browsers, native pipe unavailable, browser access host unavailable.'}}
+    (OUT / 'root_confirmation.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+    print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))

# Round 33: identity attacks and data findings

Reviewed HEAD `db1580d758062e6a4948056dc87fa42b2a04bc21`. No tracked files were edited. All private database changes used an in-memory SQLite copy. The root owns the actual CLI server on port 8933.

## M33-B1 — excluded product names re-enter through the map's broader equivalence rule (code blocker)

`src/app.py:139-144` asks the question linker whether to refuse the input, then `src/app.py:145-173` independently accepts a stored name under `_sig`. `_OTHER_PRODUCT_NAMES` is tested only inside the linker's `_marked` (`src/query.py:653-654`). The linker does not recognize every spelling which the map explicitly treats as the same name. Thus the stored forbidden trade name remains in `Engine.names`, and a missing linker hit makes the map draw it as the originator.

| Exact input | Actual HTTP map result | Plain control |
|---|---|---|
| `Myocet™`, `My/o/cet`, `My ocet` | CHEMBL359744, DOXORUBICIN HYDROCHLORIDE | `Myocet` is refused |
| `Inflectra™`, `Inf/lectra` | CHEMBL1201581, INFLIXIMAB | `Inflectra` is refused |
| `Equi/dacent` | CHEMBL1201583, BEVACIZUMAB | `Equidacent` is refused |
| `Fy/arro` | CHEMBL413, SIROLIMUS | `Fyarro` is refused |
| `Megace/ES` | CHEMBL1201139, MEGESTROL ACETATE | `Megace ES` is refused |
| `Jel/myto` | CHEMBL105, MITOMYCIN | `Jelmyto` is refused |

Every listed attack has exactly the same `_sig` as the excluded stored brand. `Myocet™` is a particularly ordinary spelling: the literal trademark must be ignored by the announced rule. This is not a free-text question-box leak. It is the relation-map endpoint drawing a product already designated as excluded. Fixing `_sig`'s trademark order for normal names did not apply the same rule to refusal membership. These cases are one code-defect family, not many independent defects.

The systematic test covers 38 actual stored spellings whose canonical key is in `_OTHER_PRODUCT_NAMES`, with 12 variants each: **456 direct map requests, 248 drawn, 201 refused, 7 excluded-biologic replies**. Drawn results span **37 base spellings**. The 248 counts by variant are trademark 36, service mark 36, slash 36, comma 36, semicolon 36, one internal space 31, and every letter spaced 37. Fullwidth, zero-width-space, registered/copyright-sign and plain controls fail closed in this cohort. [All requests](blocked_brand_variants.json), [summary](blocked_brand_summary.json), [script](brand_variants.py), [29 actual CLI HTTP captures with source records](http_attack_reproductions.json).

Independent identity controls support the difference: EMA describes Myocet as liposomal doxorubicin, separately from conventional doxorubicin ([EMA](https://www.ema.europa.eu/en/medicines/human/EPAR/myocet-liposomal)); FDA identifies Inflectra as the infliximab-dyyb biosimilar referencing Remicade ([Purple Book](https://purplebooksearch.fda.gov/index.cfm?blaNo=125544&event=productdetails)). These already-known misfiled brands are not counted again as new data findings.

## M33-D1 — accepted combination-product trade names collapse to one ingredient (data blocker)

These are exact stored names, and the map follows the current index faithfully. They therefore are data identity defects, not failures of whole-name matching. Each is an ordinary complete brand name for a product containing multiple active ingredients, whereas the drawn node names only one ingredient and exposes its graph.

The exact records below are in `data/processed/resolved.json`, under `dictionary.index[canonical_key(input)]`. **Every `source` field is literally JSON `null`, and every `alias_type` is `trade_name`.** Do not replace `null` with `ChEMBL` in the reported record. The separate provenance is the ChEMBL-id drug's `tradeNames` in `data/raw/drugs.json` (`_source: opentargets`); `src/normalize.py:171-175` imports those labels without a source argument. [Exact raw pointers and records](trade_name_raw_provenance.json), [source records, observed full HTTP response and independent references](independent_identity_references.json).

| Exact input / stored alias | Drawn node | `source` / `alias_type` | Independent identity reference |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Oraqix` | CHEMBL79 LIDOCAINE | `null` / `trade_name` | Lidocaine plus prilocaine, both 2.5%; [Dentsply label, DailyMed](https://dailymed.nlm.nih.gov/dailymed/lookup.cfm?setid=b501d239-923b-48b2-82ed-6c5c253a67e2), highlights and section 3. |
| `Actikerall` | CHEMBL185 FLUOROURACIL | `null` / `trade_name` | Fluorouracil 5 mg/g plus salicylic acid 100 mg/g; [Almirall SmPC](https://www.medicines.org.uk/emc/product/4621/smpc), section 2. |
| `Betnesol-n` | CHEMBL1200762 BETAMETHASONE SODIUM PHOSPHATE | `null` / `trade_name` | Betamethasone sodium phosphate plus neomycin sulfate; [HPRA licence PA1638/002/001](https://www.hpra.ie/find-a-medicine/for-human-use/authorised-medicines/details/28877), active substances. |
| `Maxitrol` | CHEMBL384467 DEXAMETHASONE | `null` / `trade_name` | Dexamethasone plus neomycin and polymyxin B sulfates; [Harrow label, DailyMed](https://dailymed.nlm.nih.gov/dailymed/lookup.cfm?setid=ecfb954f-5554-0ad2-e053-2995a90a7516), description/active ingredients. |
| `Tobradex` | CHEMBL384467 DEXAMETHASONE | `null` / `trade_name` | Tobramycin plus dexamethasone; [Alcon label, DailyMed](https://dailymed.nlm.nih.gov/dailymed/drugInfo.cfm?setid=2a2259ee-05e4-4785-ba75-5946132debf3). |
| `Cipro hc` | CHEMBL389621 HYDROCORTISONE | `null` / `trade_name` | Ciprofloxacin hydrochloride plus hydrocortisone; [Sandoz label, DailyMed](https://www.dailymed.nlm.nih.gov/dailymed/drugInfo.cfm?setid=b26594a3-2adf-4954-8be0-5bd6f8de9671), title and principal display panel. |

The `... component of ...` aliases in the database explicitly name a component and are not evidence for accepting the unqualified complete product as that component. These six independently supported inputs are one systemic data class; the larger screening inventory is not a proven defect count. None is an AACT-only synonym, excluded biologic synonym, previously waived development code, or graph-builder-only brand attachment.

## M33-S2 — first index occurrence hides a permitted trade-name source (nonblocking)

The code reads only `dictionary.index`, which retains one occurrence per canonical key/drug and also reflects curation. Some names have both a discarded synonym occurrence and a permitted `trade_name` occurrence in `dictionary.drugs[id].aliases`. Because the former occurrence wins the index, the map reports that the input is an excluded synonym even though a trade-name record exists.

Actual HTTP: `Darzalex`, `Tepezza`, `Tevimbra`, `Winrevair`, and `Privigen` return `biologic_synonym`; each has an exact stored `trade_name` with `source: null`. `Lmx 4` returns `registry_name`, despite an exact stored trade-name occurrence for lidocaine. The index-only source claim is therefore broader than the actual provenance. These are safe refusal/recall or explanation defects, not wrong-node blockers. [Actual HTTP and both kinds of source record](source_unicode_http.json).

A comparison finds 97 otherwise type-permitted alias records missing from the map index. **Do not count all 97 as bugs:** this includes intentional salt/parent curation and descriptive aliases removed before map construction. [Complete discrepancy candidates](alias_storage_discrepancies.json), [script](alias_sources.py). Likewise the exclusion implementation is `kind != Small molecule`, broader than antibody/protein: `PF-3512676` (Oligonucleotide), `BPI-9016M` (Unknown), and `Fraxiparine` (Oligosaccharide) receive `biologic_synonym`. This is a wording/recall limitation, not an unsafe draw.

## Unicode, source consistency, choices, retirement and IDs

The HTTP Unicode/id/source cohort has **33 exact inputs**. `Tarceva™` and `Tarceva®` now select the same hydrochloride as `Tarceva`; `trastu+zumab`, `trastuzumabγ`, `trastuzumabß`, `trastuzumab☢`, `AstraZeneca +`, `Merck + Co`, and `Pfi+zer` remain undrawn. U+200B and U+FEFF are ignored; U+034F and U+FE0F remain significant because they are class-M marks with combining class zero, rather than P/Z/C. The latter are safe recall limitations if the prose promises all invisible characters are ignored.

`ABT-510` and `FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE F 18` return complete live choices; Roche offers company and folic acid. MAGE-A3/MAGEA3 and ctla-4 now draw their target, because their drug-side synonym sources are outside the announced admitted set. This is not a remaining silent-choice bug among the admitted sources. Upper/lower-case CHEMBL IDs select the same node, an added trademark does not extend the ID path, and an ID followed by Mvasi is refused. [HTTP results](source_unicode_http.json).

A private in-memory SQLite copy exercises two genuinely ambiguous names: ABT-510 and Roche. Each is checked with both nodes live, then one retired, the retired node's ID, and both retired. The remaining live node alone is drawn; retired IDs and all-retired names are undrawn. ctla-4 is a single-candidate control. **Nine fixture observations**, no writes to the real DB. [Fixture output](retired_private_fixture.json).

The full admitted-source structural sweep is recorded separately in `admitted_source_summary.json` and `admitted_source_sweep.json` when completed. It checks whether a drawn node has a permitted stored whole-name signature and whether all live candidates under that admitted signature are returned. A pass of that structural invariant cannot validate product identity; B1 and D1 deliberately show the distinction. Screened inventory: 7,709 index alias records, including 1,214 trade-name records; [inventory](all_alias_source_records.json), [trade names](trade_names.txt), [pattern candidates](product_candidates.json).

## Candidates deliberately not promoted

`Acticin` initially looked like a different-substance alias because FDA's 2012 name-review table identifies a permethrin product. However root found contrary primary FDA evidence for a historical tretinoin gel of the same brand in NDA20-400. Therefore `Acticin -> TRETINOIN` is **not established as a wrong identity**, and is excluded from the blocker count. The FDA records are [2012 permethrin table](https://www.accessdata.fda.gov/drugsatfda_docs/nda/2012/202736Orig1s000NameR.pdf), [1998 tretinoin statistics/microbiology review](https://www.accessdata.fda.gov/drugsatfda_docs/nda/98/20400_statr_micror.pdf), and [1998 pharmacology review](https://www.accessdata.fda.gov/drugsatfda_docs/nda/98/20400_phrmr.pdf). `Depakote`, `Vistamethasone n`, `Retin-a micro`, `Ozurdex` and `Beizray` remain screened candidates here, not independent proven findings. No conclusion is inferred from unresearched candidates or from a brand's being unfamiliar.

## Spec-axis summary (under 400 words)

The source-index design now enforces whole-name membership and live candidate collection for its admitted index. The normal Trademark/Unicode, company-symbol, ambiguity and excluded registry cases address the round-32 mechanism. However the spec also says the map never draws refused products as originators. The refusal linker and map use different equivalences: `Myocet™` and `Inf/lectra` draw the originators even though `Myocet` and `Inflectra` are explicitly excluded. All these are actual map endpoint reproductions. In a 456-case brand-variant cohort, 248 results draw excluded products.

Separately, accepted exact trade names still include multi-ingredient products (Oraqix, Actikerall and four corticosteroid combinations). Six inputs are grounded in actual source records and official/manufacturer identity labels. These are data blockers and do not disprove the signature equality check. Source metadata is reported literally (`source: null`, `alias_type: trade_name`) with raw import provenance separately documented.

The admitted-name explanation is also incomplete for brands whose earlier dictionary occurrence was a synonym: Darzalex and several other stored trade names receive `biologic_synonym`, and Lmx 4 receives `registry_name`. This is a nonblocking recall/provenance defect. No production repair is proposed as completed. The evidence supports CHANGES REQUIRED regardless of the historical replay and relation/value checks.

All **38 `_OTHER_PRODUCT_NAMES` keys**, using their stored spellings, refuse as bare names and inside both `What trials evaluate NAME?` and `What does NAME target?`. No correct reading was removed in this sweep. Bare/trial/target demo examples remain unchanged. [Full differential](shared_key_question_comparison.json).

**M32 NICOTINE should-fix: FIXED.** `What trials evaluate NICOTINE?` now reads `CHEMBL3 NICOTINE`; ask, structured and check each return the same two evidence rows. The same question previously had no entities/evidence. [Exact controls](preferred_question_control.json).

**M33-S1, new question-only regression: NOT FIXED.** `What trials evaluate (+/-)NICOTINE?` and `What does (+/-)NICOTINE target?` now read **CHEMBL3 NICOTINE**, whereas the bare input `(+/-)NICOTINE` correctly reads its own preferred-name node **CHEMBL440464**. HEAD^ abstained for both questions. Each of the six current ask/structured/check requests returns evidence for CHEMBL3. The map's bare input still draws CHEMBL440464; the longer questions remain undrawn by the map. This is a question-box should-fix under the current map scope.

The source key `dictionary.index.nicotine` contains exactly `{alias: "NICOTINE", alias_type: "preferred_name", source: null, drug_id: "CHEMBL3"}` and `{alias: "(+/-)NICOTINE", alias_type: "preferred_name", source: null, drug_id: "CHEMBL440464"}`. This is a code regression, not a claim that those source assignments are wrong. At `src/query.py:887-893`, preferred-name selection is applied to the already-tokenized `NICOTINE` window after its `(+/-)` prefix has been separated. Equality of that shorter window does not establish equality to the full preferred name supplied in the question. [Source records, bare map/link and six HTTP captures](racemic_nicotine_regression.json), [parent comparison](shared_key_question_comparison.json).

# Historical cost reproductions — M32

Fifteen copied suite executions, including the isotope-instrumentation supplement, exited 0. The original2,000-piece answer was additionally exercised against the root CLI. Each reproduction and its disposition are below. Timings overlapped other audit work, so these are descriptive latencies, not an isolated benchmark or evidence of a new regression. Historical `isolated_` filenames do not imply process/host isolation. No timeout or wrong map is established by these performance checks; residual costs are should-fix items.

The implementation still runs the linker before the one-name test. Inputs over500 characters are tested as the question answer field or directly, not as successful map inputs. Shared API locking means slow question checks can delay valid maps.

|---|---|---|
| M5-S3 cubic scan, 250 x tokens / 499 characters | **FIXED** | Each predicate 2,224 calls; 33,880 aggregate input chars. [evidence](perf_current.json) |

+"""Assemble the reviewed evidence and rebase appendix links within m33 only."""
+import json
+import re
+from pathlib import Path
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+
+
+def read(name):
+    return (OUT / name).read_text(encoding='utf-8')
+
+
+def appendix(name, title):
+    content = read(name)
+    content = re.sub(r'^# .*\n', '## ' + title + '\n', content, count=1)
+    base = Path(name).parent
+    def link(m):
+        target = m[2]
+        if '://' in target or target.startswith(('#', '/')):
+            return m[0]
+        return '[' + m[1] + '](' + (base / target).as_posix() + ')'
+    return re.sub(r'\[([^\]]+)\]\(([^)]+)\)', link, content)
+
+
+def main():
+    core = read('report_core.md')
+    core = core.replace('Five separately verified', 'Six separately verified')
+    core = core.replace('four further data requests', 'five further data requests')
+    core = core.replace('**102 requests**', '**103 requests**')
+    core = core.replace(
+        'the provenance is the ChEMBL-derived `tradeNames` branch in `src/normalize.py:174-175`,',
+        'the raw `tradeNames` entries explicitly say `source: "ChEMBL"` inside the OpenTargets-sourced raw file; the normalized record loses that source argument in `src/normalize.py:174-175`,')
+    core = core.replace('[Exact records](attacks/all_alias_source_records.json),',
+        '[Exact records](attacks/all_alias_source_records.json), [raw JSON pointers and provenance](attacks/trade_name_raw_provenance.json),')
+    marker = '\nAn ingredient\'s presence'
+    extra = ('\n| M33-D6 | `Tobradex`; `Tobradex`; `tobradex` | '
+        '`CHEMBL384467` DEXAMETHASONE | The product contains **tobramycin and dexamethasone**. '
+        '[Official DailyMed label](https://dailymed.nlm.nih.gov/dailymed/drugInfo.cfm?setid=2a2259ee-05e4-4785-ba75-5946132debf3). |\n')
+    core = core.replace(marker, extra + marker)
+    core = core.replace('These records need a product-aware',
+        'The six rows are individually numbered here but constitute one systemic data class (grouped as D1 in the attack-lane notes). These records need a product-aware')
+    core = core.replace('**M33-S2 —', '**M33-S2 —')
+    core = core.replace('## Original guarantees A–G',
+        'The exclusion predicate is also broader than the antibody/protein wording: `kind != "Small molecule"` includes oligonucleotides, oligosaccharides and Unknown drugs. Exact `PF-3512676`, `Fraxiparine`, and `BPI-9016M` return `biologic_synonym`. This is a source/wording qualification, not an unsafe draw. U+034F and U+FE0F marks have combining class zero and remain significant; U+200B/U+FEFF are ignored. The exercised Unicode differences fail closed where they do not match. [Source and Unicode HTTP](attacks/source_unicode_http.json).\n\n## Original guarantees A–G')
+
+    coverage = '''
+## Historical coverage and independent source checks
+
+The naming ledger retains **275 historical finding rows**: **260 FIXED / 15 NOT FIXED overall**, and **263 FIXED / 12 NOT FIXED for the map**. Nine map rows are the recorded over-refusals; three are recall cohorts now affected by the explicit source policy, including `9-ac`/`5ac` and `RAD001C`/`rad001c`. Three other rows retain question-only failures. These statuses distinguish safe exclusion from restored recall. +# Independent relation-map review — round 33
+
+Reviewed **HEAD `db1580d758062e6a4948056dc87fa42b2a04bc21`**, parent `b21da2b29c3333af2075433043e32eeb2bb8af21`, on 2026-11-04. **CHANGES REQUIRED.** The original M32-B1–B4 and M32-D1–D3 reproductions are fixed under the revised source policy. However, the map can draw explicitly refused products when their spelling changes in a way `_sig` ignores. Five separately verified stored trade names also draw only one ingredient of a different, multi-ingredient medicinal product. These are separate code and data findings.
+
+The scope and approval bar are those in `m32/brief.md`, its original A–G guarantees, and the round-33 instructions. Question-box free-text weaknesses, existing graph-construction brand assignments, and the listed over-refusals remain nonblockers. This review makes no production repair.

+## Code blocker
+
+### M33-B1 — refusal and admission use different equivalence rules
+
+`src/app.py:59-73` includes trade names even when the question linker's `_OTHER_PRODUCT_NAMES` declares them a different product. `Engine.explore` at `src/app.py:139-150` first asks the linker to refuse the input, then admits a stored name through `_sig`. The latter ignores punctuation, spacing and trademark signs. The former depends on token windows and clause boundaries (`src/query.py:788-805,874-896`) and folds trademark signs differently (`src/query.py:117-118`). Thus an empty/non-refused linker result is not proof that the stored name is safe to draw.
+
+All following pairs were independently reproduced over HTTP. Plain controls return `refused`; the variants return `center`:
+
+| Plain, refused input | Exact variant drawn | Incorrect center |
+|---|---|---|
+| `Myocet` | `Myocet™`, `My/o/cet`, `My ocet` | `CHEMBL359744` — DOXORUBICIN HYDROCHLORIDE |
+| `Inflectra` | `Inf/lectra` | `CHEMBL1201581` — INFLIXIMAB |
+| `Equidacent` | `Equi/dacent` | `CHEMBL1201583` — BEVACIZUMAB |
+| `Fyarro` | `Fy/arro` | `CHEMBL413` — SIROLIMUS |
+| `Megace ES` | `Megace/ES` | `CHEMBL1201139` — MEGESTROL ACETATE |
+
+For example, `_sig('Myocet') == _sig('Myocet™') == _sig('My/o/cet') == 'myocet'`. This is not an extra qualifier absent from the stored name. It is a **known refused product admitted through another path**, directly violating the refusal guarantee. `_OTHER_PRODUCT_NAMES` already records that these brands denote other products; no new external product interpretation is needed to establish the code inconsistency.
+
+The systematic sweep covers **38 stored spellings × 12 forms = 456 cases**. **248 forms across 37 stored spellings draw a center**; 201 are refused and seven return an excluded-source reply. This is one failure family, not 248 separate blockers. [Sweep and exact forms](attacks/blocked_brand_variants.json), [counts](attacks/blocked_brand_summary.json), [independent attack HTTP](attacks/http_attack_reproductions.json), [root corroboration](root_confirmation.json).
+
+The necessary invariant is that equivalent spellings of a known refused stored name remain refused when the map admits names under `_sig`. Fixing only a few displayed variants would leave the differing comparison rules in place. No implementation change was made during this review.
+

+

Each response displays the indicated single-ingredient node and its relations as the identity of the named multi-ingredient medicine.

The `null` is preserved literally here: the provenance is the ChEMBL-derived `tradeNames` branch in `src/normalize.py:174-175`, not a literal `source: "ChEMBL"` field. +An ingredient's presence in a product does not make the product's whole name an identity alias for that ingredient under this review's distinct-product guarantee. These records need a product-aware curation/admission decision. Keeping them as trusted whole-name aliases still draws the wrong product even if M33-B1 is repaired.

It is excluded from the blocker count. +## New should-fix items and question-box checks
+
+**M33-S1 — the preferred-name question repair can select the wrong stereochemical record.** `(+/-)NICOTINE` by itself links and maps to its preferred-name node, **CHEMBL440464**. But **`What trials evaluate (+/-)NICOTINE?`** and **`What does (+/-)NICOTINE target?`** now link **CHEMBL3 NICOTINE**; the parent query module abstained. Symbol/token splitting leaves a shorter `NICOTINE` window, which the new preferred-name selection at `src/query.py:887-893` accepts. The three question boxes use the resulting wrong evidence; the map refuses those full questions as `not_one_name`. This is a newly introduced question-linker regression, reported as should-fix under the given scope. [Parent/current comparison and HTTP](contract/racemic_nicotine_regression.json), [root corroboration](root_shouldfix_confirmation.json).
+
+The **99-shared-key / 708-case** question comparison contains **12 changes**: ten intended preferred-name recoveries and the two racemic-nicotine question regressions. All **36 demo examples** are unchanged and their actual JavaScript callbacks pass. All **38 added product keys**, bare and in two question wrappers, are refused; **114 question-box HTTP calls** return no evidence for that product matrix. Those successful controls do not cover the map's M33-B1 equivalent-spelling bypass. [Shared-key comparison](contract/shared_key_question_comparison.json), [demos](contract/demo_previous_comparison.json), [new product checks](contract/product_questions.json).
+
+**M33-S2 — a permitted source can be hidden by an earlier excluded-source record.** `Darzalex`, `Tepezza`, `Tevimbra`, `Winrevair`, `Privigen` return `biologic_synonym`; `Lmx 4` returns `registry_name`, even though a `trade_name` record is retained in the drug's alias list. The upstream index deduplicates a spelling before the map classifies its source; `Engine.__init__` reads that index rather than all source records. This loses a permitted trade-name source and makes the reply's explanation inaccurate. It is a recall/source-classification defect, not a wrong-node blocker. The screening list of 97 source discrepancies includes intentional curated removals and is **not 97 proven defects**. [Records and actual replies](attacks/alias_storage_discrepancies.json), [root HTTP](root_shouldfix_confirmation.json).

+"""Independent round-33 HTTP confirmation. No production writes or model calls."""
+import json
+import sys
+import time
+import unicodedata
+import urllib.error
+import urllib.request
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+BASE = 'http://127.0.0.1:8933'
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'src'))
+from app import _sig
+
+
+def post(text, route='/api/explore', **extra):
+    req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + route,
+        data=json.dumps({'text': text, **extra}).encode(),
+        headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
+    start = time.perf_counter()
+    try:
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=120) as response:
+            status, body = response.status, response.read()
+    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
+        status, body = e.code, e.read()
+    return {'text': text, 'route': route, 'status': status,
+            'seconds': time.perf_counter() - start, 'body': json.loads(body)}
+
+
+def main():
+    cases = [
+        'trastu+zumab', 'trastu$zumab', 'trastu=zumab', 'trastuzumabß',
+        'trastuzumabγ', 'trastuzumab☢', 'trastuzumab²', 'AstraZeneca +',
+        'Merck + Co', 'Pfizer$', 'Pfi+zer', 'Roche $', 'Merck & Co',
+        'ABT-510', 'ABT 510', 'MAGE-A3', 'MAGEA3', 'ctla-4', 'CTLA4',
+        'FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE F 18', 'FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE-F18', 'Tarceva',
+        'Tarceva™', 'Tarceva®', 'Tarceva©', 'Tarceva℠',
+        'bupivacaine hydrochloride', 'irinotecan liposome',
+        'irinotecan liposome injection', 'nanoliposomal irinotecan',
+        'doxorubicin hydrochloride liposome', 'ropeginterferon alfa-2b',
+        '13-cis retinoic acid', 'thymosin α1', 'ifn-α-2b', 'interferon α-2b',
+        'trastuzumab™', 'AstraZeneca™', 'CT-P13', 'ABP 980', 'Zessly',
+        'MK-3475', 'MEDI4736', 'Roche', 'Kadcyla',
+        'Myocet', 'Myocet™', 'My/o/cet', 'My ocet',
+        'Inflectra', 'Inf/lectra', 'Equidacent', 'Equi/dacent',
+        'Fyarro', 'Fy/arro', 'Megace ES', 'Megace/ES',
+        'Oraqix', 'Actikerall', 'Betnesol-n', 'Depakote',
+        'osimertinib, Mvasi', 'osimertinib EGFR',
+        'chembl1201585', 'ensg00000146648', 'nct00200395',
+        'CHEMBL1201585 +', 'ＣＨＥＭＢＬ１２０１５８５',
+        'trastuzu\u200bmab', 'trastuzu\u200dmab', 'trastu\u202ezumab',
+        'trastuzumab\ufe0f', '', 'x' * 501,
+    ]
+    rows = [post(t) for t in cases]
+    rows += [post('What trials evaluate NICOTINE?', r,
+                  **({'answer': 'Nicotine is evaluated in trials.'} if r != '/api/ask' else {}))
+             for r in ['/api/ask', '/api/structured', '/api/check']]
+    (OUT / 'root_http.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+    summary = [{'text': r['text'], 'status': r['status'],
+        'center': r['body'].get('center', {}).get('id'),
+        'keys': list(r['body']),
+        'choose_ids': [c['id'] for c in r['body'].get('choose', [])]}
+        for r in rows]
+    result = {'request_count': len(rows),
+        'page_equals_worktree': urllib.request.urlopen(BASE).read() == (ROOT / 'web/try.html').read_bytes(),
+        'signature_controls': [{'original': t, 'variant': v, 'sig_original': _sig(t),
+            'sig_variant': _sig(v), 'same': _sig(t) == _sig(v)}
+            for t, v in [('Myocet','Myocet™'), ('Myocet','My/o/cet'),
+                         ('Inflectra','Inf/lectra'), ('Equidacent','Equi/dacent')]],
+        'cases': summary,
+        'browser': {'rendered_test_completed': False,
+            'reason': 'CUA createBrowserTab timed out; subsequent getState returned no apps/browsers, native pipe unavailable, browser access host unavailable.'}}
+    (OUT / 'root_confirmation.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+    print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':

# Historical replay evidence — M33

Reviewed current HEAD `db1580d758062e6a4948056dc87fa42b2a04bc21`. This lane replays earlier findings; it does not establish biochemical identity for every accepted name. New adversarial/code/data results are in the other review lanes.

## Dispositions

- All **275 inherited m1–m31 finding rows** are retained, with original source provenance, exact selected inputs and **fresh** map replies: **260 FIXED / 15 NOT FIXED** overall, **263 FIXED / 12 NOT FIXED** for map dispositions. [Full table](historical_table.md), [exact per-finding cases](per_finding_cases.json), [counts](matrix_summary.json).
- Nine map NOT FIXED rows are the previously recorded alias over-refusals. Three further rows explicitly preserve historical recall losses under the newly accepted source policy: the M6 lone-alias cohort (including `APO2L/TRAIL` and `RO 25-8310/000`), M8-S3 `9-ac`/`5ac`, and M12-S1 `RAD001C`/`rad001c`. They now return `registry_name` or `biologic_synonym`; this intentional omission is not counted as recovered recall or a new blocker. Cohort rows overlap the individual alias rows and must not be added into a unique-defect total.
- The former data-exception rows are now **FIXED for the map**: `BAT1706`, `BI 695502`, `FKB238`, `HLX-04`, `HLX04-O` return `biologic_synonym`; `13-cis retinoic acid` returns `registry_name`. The dictionary and graph were not curated.
- Three inherited question-only findings remain **NOT FIXED**, while their longer map inputs are safely undrawn: prefixed comma-name queries read `ENDOSTATIN, N-TERMINAL-MGGSHHHHH` as CHEMBL4297747 ENDOSTATIN and `Rapamycin, 42-o-(2-hydroxyethyl)-` as CHEMBL413 SIROLIMUS; `trastuzumab without any radiolabel`, `trastuzumab free from radioactive label`, and `trastuzumab compared with radioactively labelled controls` lose trial/sponsor evidence. These are observed anew, not inherited statuses. [Fresh 116-question control capture](qa_controls33.json).
- All thirteen M31-B1 exact leaks remain undrawn, with zero evidence in both original question wrappers. All six M31 clinical/prefix-ADC recall controls recover evidence arrays identical to their bare-drug controls: osimertinib 12/12, emtansine 5/5, deruxtecan 6/4. These are asserted against the fresh capture when building the ledger.

The complete **M32-B1–B4, D1–D3 and naming should-fix dispositions** are in [m32_table.md](m32_table.md). Every table input was freshly requested over HTTP on the root-owned original CLI at port8933. Code blockers B1–B4 and map data exposures D1–D3 are FIXED for the specified exact inputs. `What trials evaluate NICOTINE?` recovers CHEMBL3 and two evidence rows in ask/structured/check. Greek registry-only strings still do not draw, now with an explicit source-policy reply. Trademark recall is fixed. [Exact HTTP/source records](m32_exact_http.json).

## Limits and interpretation

The missing original M22 standalone generator remains missing; only its saved exact58-input artifact is replayed. M27/M29 are covered using their original attacks because those rounds had no completed reports. Source suites may contain controls beyond a named finding; `selection` identifies whether a ledger row uses literal mentions or an associated whole suite. Original scripts' old center expectations are not used as the current approval oracle: the independent current-source checker and observed replies determine map dispositions. Longer old positive controls are intentionally not drawn under the current one-name rule.

No production files were edited. All new files from this lane are under `audit_evidence_claims/m33/replay/`; Python bytecode and temporary locations were constrained accordingly. Source scripts inspect current modules/data and, for historical comparisons, may load only an older query module with current surrounding modules/data. Those comparisons do not isolate every historical dependency. Costs and full UI/DB/server guarantees belong to the separate contract lane; overlapping timings here are not performance-regression evidence.

+"""Assemble the reviewed evidence and rebase appendix links within m33 only."""
+import json
+import re
+from pathlib import Path
+
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+
+
+def read(name):
+    return (OUT / name).read_text(encoding='utf-8')
+
+
+def appendix(name, title):
+    content = read(name)
+    content = re.sub(r'^# .*\n', '## ' + title + '\n', content, count=1)
+    base = Path(name).parent
+    def link(m):
+        target = m[2]
+        if '://' in target or target.startswith(('#', '/')):
+            return m[0]
+        return '[' + m[1] + '](' + (base / target).as_posix() + ')'
+    return re.sub(r'\[([^\]]+)\]\(([^)]+)\)', link, content)
+
+

+# Independent relation-map review — round 33
+
+Reviewed **HEAD `db1580d758062e6a4948056dc87fa42b2a04bc21`**, parent `b21da2b29c3333af2075433043e32eeb2bb8af21`, on 2026-11-04. **CHANGES REQUIRED.** The original M32-B1–B4 and M32-D1–D3 reproductions are fixed under the revised source policy. However, the map can draw explicitly refused products when their spelling changes in a way `_sig` ignores. Five separately verified stored trade names also draw only one ingredient of a different, multi-ingredient medicinal product. These are separate code and data findings.
+
+The scope and approval bar are those in `m32/brief.md`, its original A–G guarantees, and the round-33 instructions. Question-box free-text weaknesses, existing graph-construction brand assignments, and the listed over-refusals remain nonblockers. This review makes no production repair.
+
+## Verification
+
+- Ran **`python -m pytest tests -q -p no:cacheprovider`**: **362 passed in 61.52 seconds**, no warnings. UTF-8 and bytecode suppression were set; pytest's base temporary directory and process temporary paths were under m33. PowerShell's UTF-16 output was subsequently converted to UTF-8 without changing its contents. [Output](pytest.txt).
+- Started the actual application with **`python src/app.py 8933`** and exercised its real HTTP routes. The served page equals `web/try.html` byte for byte. Root independently captured 77 baseline requests, four further data requests and 21 should-fix/control requests: **102 requests**. The parallel lanes have additional independent HTTP captures. [Baseline responses](root_http.json), [summary](root_confirmation.json), [data confirmation](root_data_confirmation.json), [should-fix confirmation](root_shouldfix_confirmation.json).
+- Checked **all 6,469 live centers against independent SQLite results**, covering **22 relation/direction/type/role combinations**, with **zero oracle errors**. All **19 DB assertions** pass, including private retirement, malformed-property, numeric and 1,001-drug chunking fixtures. [Oracle](contract/db_validate.json), [assertions](contract/db_assertions.json).
+- Executed the actual served JavaScript in a Node VM DOM/fetch harness, including real HTTP choice clicks, demos, new reply states, escaping and history. This is **not a browser rendering test**. An attempted actual browser session timed out; a fresh CUA inventory reported no available apps/browsers, an unavailable native pipe and an unavailable browser access host. No visual-layout or painted-browser result is claimed.
+- The source review, fixtures, original-script reuse and immutable-file verification are documented below and in the linked ledgers. Source fixtures use in-memory SQLite copies; read-only audit changes stay under m33. The code-change condition involving KOA Studio/KhOpenApi does not arise because no tracked implementation is edited.
+
+## Code blocker
+
+### M33-B1 — refusal and admission use different equivalence rules
+
+`src/app.py:59-73` includes trade names even when the question linker's `_OTHER_PRODUCT_NAMES` declares them a different product. `Engine.explore` at `src/app.py:139-150` first asks the linker to refuse the input, then admits a stored name through `_sig`. The latter ignores punctuation, spacing and trademark signs. The former depends on token windows and clause boundaries (`src/query.py:788-805,874-896`) and folds trademark signs differently (`src/query.py:117-118`). Thus an empty/non-refused linker result is not proof that the stored name is safe to draw.
+
+All following pairs were independently reproduced over HTTP. Plain controls return `refused`; the variants return `center`:
+
+| Plain, refused input | Exact variant drawn | Incorrect center |
+|---|---|---|
+| `Myocet` | `Myocet™`, `My/o/cet`, `My ocet` | `CHEMBL359744` — DOXORUBICIN HYDROCHLORIDE |
+| `Inflectra` | `Inf/lectra` | `CHEMBL1201581` — INFLIXIMAB |
+| `Equidacent` | `Equi/dacent` | `CHEMBL1201583` — BEVACIZUMAB |
+| `Fyarro` | `Fy/arro` | `CHEMBL413` — SIROLIMUS |
+| `Megace ES` | `Megace/ES` | `CHEMBL1201139` — MEGESTROL ACETATE |
+
+For example, `_sig('Myocet') == _sig('Myocet™') == _sig('My/o/cet') == 'myocet'`. This is not an extra qualifier absent from the stored name. It is a **known refused product admitted through another path**, directly violating the refusal guarantee. `_OTHER_PRODUCT_NAMES` already records that these brands denote other products; no new external product interpretation is needed to establish the code inconsistency.
+
+The systematic sweep covers **38 stored spellings × 12 forms = 456 cases**. **248 forms across 37 stored spellings draw a center**; 201 are refused and seven return an excluded-source reply. This is one failure family, not 248 separate blockers. [Sweep and exact forms](attacks/blocked_brand_variants.json), [counts](attacks/blocked_brand_summary.json), [independent attack HTTP](attacks/http_attack_reproductions.json), [root corroboration](root_confirmation.json).
+
+The necessary invariant is that equivalent spellings of a known refused stored name remain refused when the map admits names under `_sig`. Fixing only a few displayed variants would leave the differing comparison rules in place. No implementation change was made during this review.
+
+## Data blockers, separate from code
+
+The following are **exact stored trade-name records that the map still reads**. They are not registry-only synonyms, biologic development codes, or free-text question leaks. Each response displays the indicated single-ingredient node and its relations as the identity of the named multi-ingredient medicine.
+
+Every source record below has **`source: null`**, **`alias_type: "trade_name"`** in `data/processed/resolved.json:1`, at `dictionary.index[<key>]`. The `null` is preserved literally here: the provenance is the ChEMBL-derived `tradeNames` branch in `src/normalize.py:174-175`, not a literal `source: "ChEMBL"` field. [Exact records](attacks/all_alias_source_records.json), [root source/HTTP confirmation](root_data_confirmation.json), [additional root HTTP](root_http.json).
+
+| Finding | Exact input; alias; dictionary key | Drawn node | Independent identity reference |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| M33-D1 | `Oraqix`; `Oraqix`; `oraqix` | `CHEMBL79` LIDOCAINE | The product contains **lidocaine and prilocaine**, rather than lidocaine alone. [Official DailyMed label](https://dailymed.nlm.nih.gov/dailymed/lookup.cfm?setid=b501d239-923b-48b2-82ed-6c5c253a67e2). |
+| M33-D2 | `Actikerall`; `Actikerall`; `actikerall` | `CHEMBL185` FLUOROURACIL | Section 2 lists **fluorouracil 5 mg/g and salicylic acid 100 mg/g**. [Manufacturer SmPC](https://www.medicines.org.uk/emc/product/4621/smpc). |
+| M33-D3 | `Betnesol-n`; `Betnesol-n`; `betnesoln` | `CHEMBL1200762` BETAMETHASONE SODIUM PHOSPHATE | The regulator lists **neomycin sulfate and betamethasone sodium phosphate** as active substances. [HPRA product record](https://www.hpra.ie/find-a-medicine/for-human-use/authorised-medicines/details/28877). |
+| M33-D4 | `Maxitrol`; `Maxitrol`; `maxitrol` | `CHEMBL384467` DEXAMETHASONE | The medicinal product contains **neomycin, polymyxin B and dexamethasone**. [Official DailyMed label](https://dailymed.nlm.nih.gov/dailymed/drugInfo.cfm?setid=5776c435-ac60-49b6-8db1-23a3012b9155). |
+| M33-D5 | `Cipro HC`; `Cipro hc`; `ciprohc` | `CHEMBL389621` HYDROCORTISONE | The product contains **ciprofloxacin hydrochloride and hydrocortisone**. [Official DailyMed label](https://dailymed.nlm.nih.gov/dailymed/drugInfo.cfm?setid=6b318e8c-ce91-41ce-9f35-fc9861cd9474). |
+
+An ingredient's presence in a product does not make the product's whole name an identity alias for that ingredient under this review's distinct-product guarantee. These records need a product-aware curation/admission decision. Keeping them as trusted whole-name aliases still draws the wrong product even if M33-B1 is repaired.
+
+**Candidate not promoted to a finding:** `Acticin` draws TRETINOIN, while an FDA name-review table identifies an Acticin permethrin cream. However, separate historical FDA NDA 20-400 records explicitly call a tretinoin gel Acticin. That counterevidence prevents an unqualified claim that this stored alias is wrong. It is excluded from the blocker count. [Historical FDA statistical review](https://www.accessdata.fda.gov/drugsatfda_docs/nda/98/20400_statr_micror.pdf), [pharmacology review](https://www.accessdata.fda.gov/drugsatfda_docs/nda/98/20400_phrmr.pdf), [audit counterevidence record](root_identity_counterevidence.json). Other screened but unproven candidates are likewise not counted.

+## New should-fix items and question-box checks
+
+**M33-S1 — the preferred-name question repair can select the wrong stereochemical record.** `(+/-)NICOTINE` by itself links and maps to its preferred-name node, **CHEMBL440464**. But **`What trials evaluate (+/-)NICOTINE?`** and **`What does (+/-)NICOTINE target?`** now link **CHEMBL3 NICOTINE**; the parent query module abstained. Symbol/token splitting leaves a shorter `NICOTINE` window, which the new preferred-name selection at `src/query.py:887-893` accepts. The three question boxes use the resulting wrong evidence; the map refuses those full questions as `not_one_name`. This is a newly introduced question-linker regression, reported as should-fix under the given scope. [Parent/current comparison and HTTP](contract/racemic_nicotine_regression.json), [root corroboration](root_shouldfix_confirmation.json).
+
+The **99-shared-key / 708-case** question comparison contains **12 changes**: ten intended preferred-name recoveries and the two racemic-nicotine question regressions. All **36 demo examples** are unchanged and their actual JavaScript callbacks pass. All **38 added product keys**, bare and in two question wrappers, are refused; **114 question-box HTTP calls** return no evidence for that product matrix. Those successful controls do not cover the map's M33-B1 equivalent-spelling bypass. [Shared-key comparison](contract/shared_key_question_comparison.json), [demos](contract/demo_previous_comparison.json), [new product checks](contract/product_questions.json).
+
+**M33-S2 — a permitted source can be hidden by an earlier excluded-source record.** `Darzalex`, `Tepezza`, `Tevimbra`, `Winrevair`, `Privigen` return `biologic_synonym`; `Lmx 4` returns `registry_name`, even though a `trade_name` record is retained in the drug's alias list. The upstream index deduplicates a spelling before the map classifies its source; `Engine.__init__` reads that index rather than all source records. This loses a permitted trade-name source and makes the reply's explanation inaccurate. It is a recall/source-classification defect, not a wrong-node blocker. The screening list of 97 source discrepancies includes intentional curated removals and is **not 97 proven defects**. [Records and actual replies](attacks/alias_storage_discrepancies.json), [root HTTP](root_shouldfix_confirmation.json).

+"""Independent round-33 HTTP confirmation. No production writes or model calls."""
+import json
+import sys
+import time
+import unicodedata
+import urllib.error
+import urllib.request
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
+OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+BASE = 'http://127.0.0.1:8933'
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT / 'src'))
+from app import _sig
+
+
+def post(text, route='/api/explore', **extra):
+    req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + route,
+        data=json.dumps({'text': text, **extra}).encode(),
+        headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
+    start = time.perf_counter()
+    try:
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=120) as response:
+            status, body = response.status, response.read()
+    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
+        status, body = e.code, e.read()
+    return {'text': text, 'route': route, 'status': status,
+            'seconds': time.perf_counter() - start, 'body': json.loads(body)}
+
+
+def main():
+    cases = [
+        'trastu+zumab', 'trastu$zumab', 'trastu=zumab', 'trastuzumabß',
+        'trastuzumabγ', 'trastuzumab☢', 'trastuzumab²', 'AstraZeneca +',
+        'Merck + Co', 'Pfizer$', 'Pfi+zer', 'Roche $', 'Merck & Co',
+        'ABT-510', 'ABT 510', 'MAGE-A3', 'MAGEA3', 'ctla-4', 'CTLA4',
+        'FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE F 18', 'FLUDEOXYGLUCOSE-F18', 'Tarceva',
+        'Tarceva™', 'Tarceva®', 'Tarceva©', 'Tarceva℠',
+        'bupivacaine hydrochloride', 'irinotecan liposome',
+        'irinotecan liposome injection', 'nanoliposomal irinotecan',
+        'doxorubicin hydrochloride liposome', 'ropeginterferon alfa-2b',
+        '13-cis retinoic acid', 'thymosin α1', 'ifn-α-2b', 'interferon α-2b',
+        'trastuzumab™', 'AstraZeneca™', 'CT-P13', 'ABP 980', 'Zessly',
+        'MK-3475', 'MEDI4736', 'Roche', 'Kadcyla',
+        'Myocet', 'Myocet™', 'My/o/cet', 'My ocet',
+        'Inflectra', 'Inf/lectra', 'Equidacent', 'Equi/dacent',
+        'Fyarro', 'Fy/arro', 'Megace ES', 'Megace/ES',
+        'Oraqix', 'Actikerall', 'Betnesol-n', 'Depakote',
+        'osimertinib, Mvasi', 'osimertinib EGFR',
+        'chembl1201585', 'ensg00000146648', 'nct00200395',
+        'CHEMBL1201585 +', 'ＣＨＥＭＢＬ１２０１５８５',
+        'trastuzu\u200bmab', 'trastuzu\u200dmab', 'trastu\u202ezumab',
+        'trastuzumab\ufe0f', '', 'x' * 501,
+    ]
+    rows = [post(t) for t in cases]
+    rows += [post('What trials evaluate NICOTINE?', r,
+                  **({'answer': 'Nicotine is evaluated in trials.'} if r != '/api/ask' else {}))
+             for r in ['/api/ask', '/api/structured', '/api/check']]
+    (OUT / 'root_http.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+    summary = [{'text': r['text'], 'status': r['status'],
+        'center': r['body'].get('center', {}).get('id'),
+        'keys': list(r['body']),
+        'choose_ids': [c['id'] for c in r['body'].get('choose', [])]}
+        for r in rows]
+    result = {'request_count': len(rows),
+        'page_equals_worktree': urllib.request.urlopen(BASE).read() == (ROOT / 'web/try.html').read_bytes(),
+        'signature_controls': [{'original': t, 'variant': v, 'sig_original': _sig(t),
+            'sig_variant': _sig(v), 'same': _sig(t) == _sig(v)}
+            for t, v in [('Myocet','Myocet™'), ('Myocet','My/o/cet'),
+                         ('Inflectra','Inf/lectra'), ('Equidacent','Equi/dacent')]],
+        'cases': summary,
+        'browser': {'rendered_test_completed': False,
+            'reason': 'CUA createBrowserTab timed out; subsequent getState returned no apps/browsers, native pipe unavailable, browser access host unavailable.'}}
+    (OUT / 'root_confirmation.json').write_text(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding='utf-8')
+    print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
+
+
+if __name__ == '__main__':
+    main()
